Every active route and every reachable state was rendered in a browser and the live DOM walked in document order: text, [aria-label], [alt], [title], [placeholder], resolved aria-labelledby / aria-describedby, control states, live regions; "(hidden)" marks text in the page but not shown until an interaction.
The generated sent-journey text, the email backup, the seat tickets and travel passes (PDF text read back) and the plan words are included. Exact-duplicate global blocks are written once as G-nnn and referenced with USES G-nnn; every variant is written in full.
No access code appears: [SECRET ACCESS CODE OMITTED] stands where one would be typed or shown. Contacts are test values. Nothing is summarised, corrected or shortened.

State Railway of Thailand
1 / 9
Transportation · State Railway of Thailand
Special Express No. 25
24 – 25 February 2027 · Bangkok → Nong Khai → Vientiane
The whole party leaves Bangkok together at dusk and wakes on the Mekong. First Class on the State Railway of Thailand means a private lockable cabin rather than a seat: two berths, a door you close behind you, and a lower bed that folds back into two facing seats for the evening. It is the one night of the journey that is also the journey.
The journey
Service
Special Express No. 25 · State Railway of Thailand
Route
Bangkok (Krung Thep Aphiwat) → Nong Khai, then onward to Vientiane by road
Departure
24 February 2027 · 20:25 · Krung Thep Aphiwat Central Terminal
Arrival
25 February 2027 · 06:25 · Nong Khai
On board
Approximately 10 hours
Class
First Class Sleeper · private cabin
Cabin
2 berths · upper and lower
What the journey gives you
Your cabin
A private cabin with a lockable door
Two berths — one upper, one lower
The lower berth converts into two facing seats by day
Single occupancy where the carriage allows it
Compartments for bags and personal items
Comfort
Air conditioning with individual control
Reading lights
Foldable table and mirror
In-suite washbasin in every cabin
Power and USB outlets
Sleep
Bedding and pillows
Clean linen
Blankets
The berth is made up for you by the attendant
Bathrooms
Shared western-style toilets, just outside the cabins
A shared hot-water shower in the CNR First Class carriage
Dining
Meals ordered from the cabin and brought to you by the train staff
A dining car on board
Thai and international selections
Vegetarian options
Service & support
Attendant service throughout the night
Bedding set up and cleared for you
Staff on hand for assistance for the length of the journey
Guest Relations travels with the party
What is included
One berth in a First Class Sleeper cabin, Bangkok to Nong Khai.
Dinner on board and breakfast the next morning.
The border-crossing documentation prepared for you.
The coordinated onward transfer from Nong Khai across the border to Souphattra Heritage in Vientiane.
What you arrange yourself
Anything ordered on board beyond the included dinner and breakfast.
Personal expenses at the terminal or at the border.
Arrival & transfer
Nong Khai, early morning: the party is met and travels on together by road.
The border formalities at the Thai–Lao Friendship Bridge are prepared in advance; each guest still presents themselves in person.
The road continues to Souphattra Heritage Vientiane, where the Pre-Wedding Stay begins.
Good to know
The train carries a single First Class carriage of twelve lockable cabins — twenty-four berths in total. Cabins are held for the party.
This is an overnight service: the evening is spent on board and the arrival is at daybreak.
Guests travelling as a pair share one cabin; the arrangement is made with Guest Relations.
The other legs of the journey

Kunming → Lijiang

Bangkok · Lunch · Bangkok days
Sühring
Welcome to Sühring, a home of stories told through food. A villa, two brothers, and countless memories.
From the hosts’ notes on the place
About Sühring
[state] expanded
The philosophy
Timeless techniques.
+
Childhood recipes and modern creativity come together on every plate. It begins with curiosity, continues with surprise, and ends with the warmth of belonging.
The founders
Tradition & innovation.
+
(hidden) Experience the culinary delights of award-winning chefs Thomas and Mathias Sühring, owners of Sühring, a three-Michelin-starred modern German restaurant in the heart of Bangkok.
The foundation
Philosophy & approach.
+
(hidden) Our family is undeniably the foundation of who we have become today. It has given us examples of love, behavior, and values that continue to shape our identity. The kitchen was always the heart of our home, and we are especially grateful to our grandmother, who showed us through her devotion to cooking how magical food can be.
(hidden) — Mathias and Thomas Sühring
The first mentor
Our inspiration.
+
(hidden) Our grandmother Christa, herself a trained chef, was our first mentor. She showed us that food could be both humble and refined. On her farm just outside Berlin, she taught us the beauty of the seasons and of the ingredients she grew.
(hidden) Driven by her passion for cooking, she created a warm, welcoming environment where everyday family meals became cherished, lasting memories. That philosophy has stayed with us ever since, and it shapes every menu we write.
Contemporary heritage
The kitchen.
+
(hidden) Today, we draw inspiration from cherished family recipes, childhood memories, and years of travel. Our cooking reinterprets the rich traditions of German cuisine with a contemporary twist, emphasizing technique, refinement, and a deep respect for the ingredients we work with.
Practical information
Price
USD 180 per person
For each participating guest, as decided by the hosts.
Opening hours
Lunch Thursday to Sunday 12:30 pm to 13:00 pm (last seating) Closed on Monday and Tuesday
Where
Map
Website

(hidden) Your journey has been received by Guest Relations
(hidden) Guest Relations will review your selections personally. Nothing is confirmed yet; we will confirm each arrangement with you once it is ready.
(hidden) [aria-describedby → spoken] Everything we need is hereYour journey can be sentAnything optional can follow at any time — it never delays your journey.
(hidden) Send to Guest Relations
(hidden) [state] live region · polite · role alert
(hidden) Sending shares your journey with Guest Relations so a person can look at it. It is a request, never a payment, and it is not a confirmation — Guest Relations confirms every arrangement with you personally.
(hidden) What happens now
(hidden) Received means your journey is safely with Guest Relations. Confirmed is something only Guest Relations can tell you, once each arrangement is held with the hotels, the railway and the airlines. Until you hear from them, nothing in your journey is final.
(hidden) You can keep changing anything here. Change it, send it again, and the newer version replaces the earlier one.

One invitation. Three countries. One journey.
Your Invitation
Thailand, Laos and China — prepared around you.
21 February – 8 March 2027. Bangkok before the wedding, Vientiane at its heart, Kunming and Lijiang after — then two final nights back in Bangkok. The complete journey opens with the private code from your invitation.

[head]
[page title] Destinations · See You In Laos
USES G-001 (header)
[page]
[aria-label] The Mekong promenade in Vientiane at sunset
Destinations
Thailand, Laos, China.
21 February – 8 March 2027
Three countries, one continuous journey.
Bangkok opens the journey. Vientiane holds the wedding at its centre. Kunming and Lijiang follow, before two final nights back in Bangkok.
[aria-label] The Chao Phraya river and the Bangkok skyline
01 · Thailand
Bangkok
The shared city days that open the journey — the river at golden hour, the temples of the old town, and the night train waiting at Krung Thep Aphiwat to go north. The journey returns here for its last two nights.
Journeys calling here
[aria-label] The Thai–Lao Friendship Bridge at Nong Khai
[aria-label] Patuxai and the fountain park in Vientiane at twilight
02 · Laos
Vientiane
The quiet capital on the Mekong: a French avenue that ends in a triumphal arch, the golden stupa of Pha That Luang, and a riverfront that fills every evening. This is where the whole journey leans — the wedding day is here.
The wedding days
[aria-label] Pha That Luang, the golden stupa of Vientiane
[aria-label] Lane Xang Avenue, Vientiane
Pha That Luang · Lane Xang Avenue, Vientiane
[aria-label] Temple roofs and cherry blossom in Kunming
03 · China
Kunming & Lijiang
Kunming first — the city of eternal spring, cherry blossom in the temple courtyards, three nights before the train. Then Lijiang: the Naxi old town below Jade Dragon Snow Mountain, and the water that runs through every lane.
Journeys calling here
[aria-label] Naxi rooftops in Lijiang below the snow mountain
[aria-label] Black Dragon Pool, Lijiang
Lijiang old town · Black Dragon Pool
Your Invitation
Every stage, chosen by you.

Experiences
The days between the days.

USES G-008 (head)
USES G-001 (header)
[page]
[aria-label] The green gateway at Souphattra Heritage, Vientiane
Sunday, 28 February 2027 · Vientiane, Laos
The Wedding
Stay 27 February – 1 March 2027
One day, four moments.
The temple in the morning, coffee and cake on the return, the vows at the green door and dinner poolside. Coffee and cake, the vows and the dinner are hosted for every guest — part of your journey, never a charge. Buddhist giving at the temple is personal and self-pay.
Choose your wedding stay
[aria-label] Novice monks with their alms bowls in the morning, and a guest kneeling in greeting
08:00 – 12:00 · Wat Ong Teu, Vientiane
Temple Ceremony
The wedding day begins at the temple. A Buddhist morning ceremony, unhurried, with time to take part in the giving that opens the day. Afterwards we return together to the hotel.
Dress · Lao Traditional Dress
View dress code
Coming to the temple is your own decision, and we would like to know it in advance — so that the morning is prepared for the guests who are there.
Tell us your decision
[aria-label] Haruthai and Suthep kneeling in Lao traditional dress, placing food in a novice monk's alms bowl during the morning offering
Part of the morning · Tak Bat / Sai Bat
Morning Alms-Giving
Guests attending the Temple Ceremony are invited to take part in the traditional morning alms-giving, Tak Bat. This is the moment in which food is respectfully offered to Buddhist monks — one of the oldest ways a day begins in Laos. It is self-pay: each guest arranges their own small offering of food on the morning itself. No amount is set here.
Part of the Temple Ceremony · self-pay
[aria-label] The morning alms-giving
[aria-label] Novice monks waiting with their alms bowls
[aria-label] Haruthai offering food into a monk's alms bowl, Suthep kneeling beside her, a second monk passing behind
The morning alms-giving · Tak Bat
[aria-label] Prepared Sangkhathan offerings in woven baskets, tied with ribbon
Sangkhathan · Optional · USD 15 per guest
Sangkhathan Temple Offering
A personal offering of useful everyday items for the monastic community, presented by you. It is not the morning alms-giving: Tak Bat is the food offered during the ceremony, arranged and paid for by each guest (self-pay), while the Sangkhathan is a separate, optional offering that you may choose to have prepared in your name.
We will prepare the offering for you. In keeping with the personal meaning of the ritual, each participating guest contributes to and presents their own offering personally.
Prepared offering Daily-use amenities, personal-care items and other appropriate everyday necessities for the monastic community.
If it applies to your invitation, you will find it in your private journey
Your private journey
Who is joining which part of the day?
Every guest on an invitation answers the wedding day for themselves — the Temple Ceremony, the Sangkhathan, Coffee & Cake, the Vow Ceremony and the Wedding Dinner. Those answers are made inside your own private journey, where they are kept by name.
Open your private journey

ROUTE: /dress
STATE: PUBLIC · DEFAULT · signed out · 390 px
SOURCE: dress.html
CONTEXT: dress code (direct address only)

[head]
[page title] Dress Code · See You In Laos
USES G-001 (header)
[page]
Dress Code
Three codes · one wedding day · twelve days of travel
The wedding day carries two dress codes, and the travelling days carry a third. The references below are photographs, not rules: they show the register, not a uniform. Nothing is bought or hired through this website.
Temple Ceremony
Lao Traditional Dress
Coffee & Cake
Black Tie
Vow Ceremony
Black Tie
Wedding Dinner
Black Tie
The travelling days
Resort Wear
Sunday, 28 February 2027 · Morning
Lao Traditional Dress
Temple Ceremony · 08:00 – 12:00 · Wat Ong Teu, Vientiane
The Buddhist morning is dressed in Lao tradition. Shoulders and knees are covered inside the temple, and shoes come off at the door. A sinh may be borrowed or made in Vientiane — Guest Relations helps anyone who would like that arranged before the morning.
[aria-label] Lao traditional dress references
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 1
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 2
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 3
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 4
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 5
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 6
Six references · Lao traditional dress
Sunday, 28 February 2027 · Afternoon and evening
Black Tie
Coffee & Cake · Vow Ceremony · Wedding Dinner · Souphattra Heritage, Vientiane
From the return out of the temple, the day is black tie and stays black tie until the end of dinner. The courtyard is warm and the evening is long: a dinner jacket, a long dress, and shoes that survive a garden are the whole of it.

Six references · black tie
21 February – 08 March 2027
Resort Wear
The travelling days · Bangkok · the night train · Vientiane · Kunming · Lijiang
Everything outside the wedding day is resort wear — easy, unhurried, and warm enough for a Lijiang evening at 2,400 metres. Comfortable shoes matter more than anything else on this list.

Six references · resort wear
In your private journey
Each of you acknowledges the dress code, in your own name
The acknowledgement is part of step 04 · Wedding Preparation of your private journey. It is never ticked for you, and your journey cannot be sent until each named guest has reviewed it.

The main bedroom
1 / 7
Suite · Souphattra Heritage Vientiane
Souphattra Presidential
The largest suite of the house: two bedrooms, private bathrooms and a shared living space under a high ceiling.
Only one Presidential exists in the house. Two bedrooms, each with its own bathroom — a king in one, twins in the other — open onto a separate living area and a shared co-living space beneath a high ceiling. A pantry and a dining table make it the suite a family gathers in rather than passes through.
The Room
Size
118 sq.m.
Bed
Two bedrooms · 1 king bed and twin beds
Occupancy
4 adults · 2 children
Location
2nd floor · one unit only
Bathrooms
Two private bathrooms
Comfort & amenities
The room
118 sq.m., the only one in the house
Two bedrooms, each with its own private bathroom
King bed and twin beds
Separate living area and shared co-living space
High ceiling
Pantry and dining table
Bathroom
Private bathroom · bathtub or shower
Hot water, 24 hours
Bathrobes, slippers and towels
Hair dryer and vanity mirror
Full toiletries
Bidet sprayer
Accessible shower

Minibar — complimentary
Bottled water and soft drinks — complimentary
Nespresso machine, coffee and tea

Technology

Smart door lock
Telephone

Service
Daily housekeeping
Turndown service
Safe in the room
Blackout curtains and down duvet
Sewing kit and umbrella
Air conditioning with individual control
What is included
Pre-Wedding Stay (25 – 27 February): two nights, 25 → 26 and 26 → 27 February. Both nights at your room rate.
Wedding Stay (27 February – 1 March): two nights, 27 → 28 February and 28 February → 1 March. The first night at your room rate; the second night hosted by Haruthai & Suthep.
The two stays run back to back — 27 February is the transition day, and no night between 25 February and 1 March is left uncovered.
Each window is a fixed two-night window: arriving late or leaving early does not change the amount.
Breakfast is included on every morning.
A limited number of complimentary alternative stays are also available.

Souphattra Heritage Vientiane sits at the heart of our wedding stay: shared mornings, shared arrivals, and the rhythm of the weekend centred around one quiet place. Choose the stay that feels right for you.

Souphattra Majestic Suite
The house suite: a separate living area, pantry and bar, and a long balcony over the pool.
Eighty-four square metres at the summit of the resort. A separate living area with its own pantry and bar sits beside the bedroom, and the balcony runs the length of the suite with the pool and the garden below it. Lao contemporary lines under French colonial ceilings — the room the house was built around.

Suite · Souphattra Heritage Vientiane
Noble Courtyard Suite
A 63 square metre retreat with a King bed, two bathrooms, a separate living area and a private balcony overlooking the garden and pool.
Sixty-three square metres arranged for two people who like their own space: a king bed, a separate living area with a sofa and a Smart TV, and — unusually — two bathrooms and two shower rooms, one each. It sits on the ground floor in the middle of the garden, with the balcony opening onto the greenery and the pool.

Deluxe Balcony King
Deluxe Balcony King Room Non Smoking — the coordinated return stay in Bangkok.
The Royal Wing, eighth floor. The balcony looks over the hotel’s lawns and water features with seating set out on it; inside there is a generous living area, a working desk and a marble bathroom with a walk-in rain shower. Two breakfasts are included, and the minibar is complimentary.

Business Class · China Eastern
1 / 3
Transportation · China Eastern Airlines
MU9646 · Vientiane → Kunming
01 March 2027 · Vientiane → Kunming
The wedding days end and the journey turns north. One non-stop flight out of Vientiane at ten to four, an hour and thirty-five minutes in the air, and the party is in Kunming before dinner — the city that calls itself eternal spring, and means it. Business Class is the arrangement we hold; an Economy Flexible fare is offered beside it for anyone who would rather spend the difference elsewhere.
The journey
Flight
MU9646 · China Eastern Airlines
Route
Vientiane (VTE) → Kunming (KMG)
Date
01 March 2027
Departure

Arrival
18:25 · Kunming (KMG)
Routing
Non-stop · 1 hour 35 minutes
Aircraft
Boeing 738 narrowbody
Class
Business Class, or Economy Flexible
What the journey gives you
Your seat
One Business Class seat per guest
Business Class check-in and boarding
Times shown are local at each airport
On board
A meal is served in Business Class on this service
Cabin service throughout the flight
Baggage
Two pieces of checked baggage, included
Cabin baggage to the operator’s Business Class allowance
Fare conditions
Free rescheduling before departure
Conditional ticket refund before departure
Changes are made through Guest Relations, never on this website
What is included
One Business Class seat, Vientiane to Kunming, non-stop.
Two pieces of checked baggage.
The meal service carried in Business Class on this flight.
What you arrange yourself
Airport transfers at either end.
Anything purchased at the airport.
Arrival & transfer
Departure from Vientiane is at 15:50 on 1 March, after the Wedding Stay ends.
Arrival in Kunming is the same afternoon, in time for the first night at Wanxiang Yueju.
Transfers between the airports and the hotels are arranged by each guest.
Good to know
The flight number, times and fare shown here are the Owner’s confirmed production arrangement for the party.
Seats are held as a block; Guest Relations confirms each name.

Transportation · China Railway
C86 · Kunming → Lijiang
04 March 2027 · Kunming → Lijiang
Five hundred and twenty-seven kilometres of Yunnan in one morning: out of Kunming at a quarter past ten, across the gorges, and into Lijiang in the early afternoon, in Business Class.

Seating arrangements can vary by train set.
Good to know
The line cut the Kunming–Lijiang journey from about seven hours to about three and a half; this service runs at up to 200 km/h.

Transportation · China Eastern Airlines
MU5924 + MU741 · Lijiang → Bangkok
06 March 2027 · Lijiang → Kunming → Bangkok
The way home, in two hops and one morning. Out of Lijiang just after ten, ninety minutes on the ground in Kunming, and into Bangkok early in the afternoon — with two closing nights still to come.

SURFACE 063
==================================================
ROUTE: /marsilea · spa etiquette
STATE: EACH NOTE OPENED
SOURCE: marsilea.html
CONTEXT: the etiquette notes

[state] expanded
Arrival
−
We recommend that you check in at the spa reception at least 15 minutes prior to your first scheduled appointment. This allows us to have a discussion with you about your treatment expectations. Please understand that late arrivals will not receive an extension of scheduled treatments.
[state] expanded
Advance Booking
−
We highly recommend booking your treatment in advance to ensure that your preferred time and service is available. This also applies to group classes.
[state] expanded
Age Requirement
−
The minimum age requirement for access to the Spa is 15 and Fitness Center is 18. Children under 18 must be accompanied by a responsible adult when in the Spa, Gym, Swimming Pool, Steam & Sauna.
[state] expanded
Cancellation
−
Please allow 4 hours’ notice of cancellation to avoid a 50% charge, and 100% charge for no shows; for non-hotel guests, a credit card number is required at the time of booking.
[state] expanded
Cellular Telephone
−
Noise pollution adds to our everyday stress. In order to ensure tranquility and relaxation, we request you to kindly turn off your cell phone.
[state] expanded
Gratuity
−
The price includes only 10% government tax and 10% service charge. Personal gratuities are at your discretion.
[state] expanded
Health Conditions
−
Please advise us of any health conditions, allergies, or injuries, which could affect your treatment when making your spa reservation.
[state] expanded
No Smoking & Alcohol
−
To maintain a serene and healthy environment for all our guests, we kindly ask that you refrain from smoking or consuming alcohol within the spa premises.
[state] expanded
Pregnancy
−
We have specially designed treatments for expectant mothers. Please allow our reception to guide you in selecting which treatments are most suitable for you during this special time.

Vientiane · Experience · Gold & sacred
Pha That Luang
The golden stupa — Laos' national symbol, radiant in the morning.
From the hosts' itinerary · Day 05 · 25.02.2027
About
The great golden stupa is the national symbol of Laos — about 3.5 kilometres from the centre, its gilded spire visible long before you arrive.
Give it an unhurried hour: the cloistered courtyard, the reclining Buddha beside the stupa, and gold that shifts with every change of light.

[aria-label] Carousel position
[state] live region · polite
1 / 2
[aria-label] Previous photograph
[state] disabled
[aria-label] Next photograph
Vientiane · Experience · City & architecture
Patuxai
Vientiane's triumphal arch — palm-lined, unhurried, and worth the climb for the view down the avenue.
From the hosts’ Vientiane portrait
About
Raised in the late 1950s and 1960s, the arch answers Paris with Lao form — naga finials, lotus mouldings and a crown of five towers above the city’s grandest avenue.
Climb it: the upper terraces look straight over the fountain park and down Lane Xang Avenue — our second photograph is that view. Morning and late afternoon bring the kindest light.

Vientiane · Experience · Shopping & places · Mekong & evening
Vientiane Night Market
Red roofs along the Mekong at dusk — lanterns, stalls and the city out for the evening.
From the hosts' itinerary · Day 06 · 26.02.2027
About
Every evening the red-roofed stalls open along Chao Anouvong Park — clothes, crafts and small food, with the Mekong turning to colour behind them.
Come for the promenade as much as the market: at sunset the riverfront fills with families, runners and food carts, and the far bank is already Thailand.

[aria-label] Carousel position

[state] disabled
[aria-label] Next photograph
Kunming · Yunnan · Experience · Seasonal
Kunming Cherry Blossoms
In spring, Kunming's gardens shift into layers of pink blossom, water, stone paths and traditional architecture.
From the hosts' itinerary · Day 09 – 10 · 01.–02.03.2027
About
The most atmospheric locations bring together Yunnan cherry blossoms and flowering begonia with streams, small cascades, white walls, dark tiled roofs and planted walkways — less a single attraction than a seasonal landscape that changes with light, weather and the stage of bloom.
Seasonal note — Kunming's main cherry blossom season usually builds from mid-March into April. Our early-March visit may fall before peak bloom, so actual flowering conditions will depend on the season.
Yuantongshan (Wuhua District) — the classic Kunming blossom garden: Yunnan cherry, weeping begonia and a dense pink canopy in an established city park.
Kunming suburban park (Xishan District) — a broader, quieter garden landscape for more spacious, immersive flower viewing.
Best light — mornings 09:00–11:00 and late afternoons 16:00–18:00; midday light is harsh for photography. Perspectives worth seeking: hanging branches, water and blossom layers, stone-path depth, white-wall framing, backlit petals.
Good to know — bloom timing varies by year and weather; spring temperatures shift and Yunnan sun can be strong; allow two to three unhurried hours; please leave branches and planting untouched.
All experiences

USES G-003 (footer)

02 · 24 – 25 FEB
Bangkok → Vientiane
Transportation
[aria-label] Special Express No. 25
Transportation · State Railway of Thailand
Special Express No. 25
Bangkok after dark. Nong Khai at first light. The overnight train carries you north in a private First Class sleeper cabin; from Nong Khai, Guest Relations coordinates the onward journey across the border to Vientiane.
A private, lockable cabin with two berths
Van and border logistics on to Vientiane, included
Bedding, air conditioning and power at the berth

per person · package · your cost

Your cost
Your cost
USD 0
Vientiane is two consecutive stays. The Pre-Wedding Stay covers 25 → 26 and 26 → 27 February, both nights your room rate. The Wedding Stay covers 27 → 28 February and 28 February → 01 March as one selection: the first night your room rate, the second night hosted by Haruthai & Suthep. The hosted wedding day is part of your journey without charge. A spa interest carries no journey cost — paid directly at the spa.

Sunday, 28 February 2027 · Vientiane
The Wedding
One day, four moments. The temple in the morning. Coffee & Cake on the return. The vows at the green door. Dinner poolside. Coffee & Cake, the Vow Ceremony and the Wedding Dinner are hosted by Haruthai & Suthep; Tak Bat remains a personal offering at the temple. Every answer here is yours alone.
Your invitation
Open your invitation to answer
The wedding day is answered in your own name, so we open your invitation first.

CONTEXT: the gate

USES G-039 (head)

USES G-023 (shell bar)
[page]
Sunday, 28 February 2027 · and the travelling days
Wedding Preparation
Two things to prepare for the wedding day: what to wear, and where you will sit. The dress code is understood once, here, and confirmed once — in your own name.
01 · Required
Dress code
The wedding day carries two codes, and the travelling days carry a third. The photographs are references, not rules: they show the register, not a uniform. Nothing is bought or hired through this website.
Sunday, 28 February · Morning
Lao Traditional Dress
Temple Ceremony · 08:00 – 12:00 · Wat Ong Teu, Vientiane
The Buddhist morning is dressed in Lao tradition. Shoulders and knees are covered inside the temple, and shoes come off at the door. A sinh may be borrowed or made in Vientiane — Guest Relations helps anyone who would like that arranged.
[aria-label] Lao traditional dress references
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 1
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 2
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 3
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 4
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 5
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 6
Sunday, 28 February · Afternoon and evening
Black Tie
Coffee & Cake · Vow Ceremony · Wedding Dinner · Souphattra Heritage
From the return out of the temple, the day is black tie and stays black tie until the end of dinner. The courtyard is warm and the evening is long: a dinner jacket, a long dress, and shoes that survive a garden are the whole of it.
[aria-label] Black tie references
[alt] Black tie — reference 1
[alt] Black tie — reference 2
[alt] Black tie — reference 3
[alt] Black tie — reference 4
[alt] Black tie — reference 5
[alt] Black tie — reference 6
21 February – 08 March
Resort Wear
The travelling days · Bangkok · the night train · Vientiane · Kunming · Lijiang
Everything outside the wedding day is resort wear — easy, unhurried, and warm enough for a Lijiang evening at 2,400 metres. Comfortable shoes matter more than anything else on this list.
[aria-label] Resort wear references

[alt] Resort wear — reference 4
[alt] Resort wear — reference 5
[alt] Resort wear — reference 6

(hidden) Received
(hidden) Your journey has been received by Guest Relations
(hidden) Guest Relations will review your selections personally. Nothing is confirmed yet; we will confirm each arrangement with you once it is ready.
[aria-describedby → spoken] REQUIRED · Open your invitation · Your journey is sent under your own invitation, so we open it first. · Your invitation · OPEN
Send to Guest Relations — not ready yet
[state] live region · polite · role alert
Sending shares your journey with Guest Relations so a person can look at it. It is a request, never a payment, and it is not a confirmation — Guest Relations confirms every arrangement with you personally.
(hidden) What happens now
(hidden) Received means your journey is safely with Guest Relations. Confirmed is something only Guest Relations can tell you, once each arrangement is held with the hotels, the railway and the airlines. Until you hear from them, nothing in your journey is final.
(hidden) You can keep changing anything here. Change it, send it again, and the newer version replaces the earlier one.

Complete journey
Complete the remaining journey.
Every stage you have already chosen stays exactly as you chose it. The complete journey fills only the stages that are still open — each with the room, cabin or seat suggested for the whole journey — and holds your place in a room where someone from your party already is, whenever there is one. You can still change any stage afterwards.

Special Express No. 25
Bangkok after dark. Nong Khai at first light. The overnight train carries you north in a private First Class sleeper cabin; from Nong Khai, Guest Relations coordinates the onward journey across the border to Vientiane.

Your cost
Your cost

Vientiane is two consecutive stays. The Pre-Wedding Stay covers 25 → 26 and 26 → 27 February, both nights your room rate. The Wedding Stay covers 27 → 28 February and 28 February → 01 March as one selection: the first night your room rate, the second night hosted by Haruthai & Suthep. The hosted wedding day is part of your journey without charge. A spa interest carries no journey cost — paid directly at the spa.

Heritage Room · Souphattra Heritage Vientiane
The Heritage
Colonial French elegance in 31 square metres, with a private balcony over the garden.
Thirty-one square metres of colonial French elegance with a private balcony over the garden — the room the house is named for. Two guests, top-tier comfort, and a minibar replenished at no charge for the length of the stay.

Wedding Stay (27 February – 1 March): two nights, 27 → 28 February and 28 February → 1 March. The first night at your room rate; the second night hosted by Haruthai & Suthep.
The two stays run back to back — 27 February is the transition day, and no night between 25 February and 1 March is left uncovered.
Each window is a fixed two-night window: arriving late or leaving early does not change the amount.
Breakfast is included on every morning.
A limited number of complimentary alternative stays are also available.

Noble Courtyard Suite

The double-height living room
1 / 11
Whole home · six bedrooms · Bangkok · Before the Wedding
Sathorn Penthouse
The shared days in Bangkok before travelling on to Laos — one penthouse for the whole party, capacity 12 adults.
A whole house rather than a hotel floor: 162 square metres across the fourth and fifth storeys, six bedrooms, a double-height living room and a garden balcony. A private elevator with its own security system opens directly into the living floor. The kitchen is a real one, the Wi-Fi is measured at 710 Mbps, and there is a Casiotone in the corner for whoever gets there first.

Below Jade Dragon Snow Mountain
1 / 6
Transportation · China Railway
C86 · Kunming → Lijiang
04 March 2027 · Kunming → Lijiang
Five hundred and twenty-seven kilometres of Yunnan in one morning: out of Kunming at a quarter past ten, across the gorges, and into Lijiang in the early afternoon, in Business Class.
The journey
Service
C86 · high-speed train
Route
Kunming Railway Station → Lijiang Railway Station
Date
04 March 2027
Departure
10:15 · Kunming
Arrival
13:44 · Lijiang
Routing
Direct · 3 hours 29 minutes
Distance
About 527 kilometres
Class
Business Class
What the journey gives you
Your seat
Spacious seat pitch
Adjustable backrest
Tray table
Luggage rack
Comfort
Wireless charging
Power outlet at the seat
Air conditioning
Bathroom and hot-water supply on board
Slippers, blanket and eye mask, subject to availability
Dining
Complimentary snacks and drinks served at the seat
Availability follows the service on the day
Priority Ticketing
Expedited ticketing and shorter wait times.
What is included
One Business Class seat, Kunming to Lijiang, direct.
Complimentary drinks and snacks at the seat.
Priority Ticketing: expedited ticketing and shorter wait times.
What you arrange yourself
Transfers to Kunming Railway Station and from Lijiang Railway Station.
Anything purchased on board beyond the included service.
Arrival & transfer
Kunming Railway Station is in Guandu District; guests make their own way there from the hotel.
The train arrives at Lijiang Railway Station at 13:44; the road on to Baisha is arranged by each guest.
Seating arrangements can vary by train set.
Good to know
The line cut the Kunming–Lijiang journey from about seven hours to about three and a half; this service runs at up to 200 km/h.
The train number, times and fare shown here are the Owner’s confirmed production arrangement for the party.

Bangkok · Lunch · Bangkok days
Sühring
Welcome to Sühring, a home of stories told through food. A villa, two brothers, and countless memories.
From the hosts’ notes on the place
About Sühring
[state] expanded
The philosophy
Timeless techniques.
+
Childhood recipes and modern creativity come together on every plate. It begins with curiosity, continues with surprise, and ends with the warmth of belonging.
The founders
Tradition & innovation.
+
(hidden) Experience the culinary delights of award-winning chefs Thomas and Mathias Sühring, owners of Sühring, a three-Michelin-starred modern German restaurant in the heart of Bangkok.
The foundation
Philosophy & approach.
+
(hidden) Our family is undeniably the foundation of who we have become today. It has given us examples of love, behavior, and values that continue to shape our identity. The kitchen was always the heart of our home, and we are especially grateful to our grandmother, who showed us through her devotion to cooking how magical food can be.
(hidden) — Mathias and Thomas Sühring
The first mentor
Our inspiration.
+
(hidden) Our grandmother Christa, herself a trained chef, was our first mentor. She showed us that food could be both humble and refined. On her farm just outside Berlin, she taught us the beauty of the seasons and of the ingredients she grew.
(hidden) Driven by her passion for cooking, she created a warm, welcoming environment where everyday family meals became cherished, lasting memories. That philosophy has stayed with us ever since, and it shapes every menu we write.
Contemporary heritage
The kitchen.
+
(hidden) Today, we draw inspiration from cherished family recipes, childhood memories, and years of travel. Our cooking reinterprets the rich traditions of German cuisine with a contemporary twist, emphasizing technique, refinement, and a deep respect for the ingredients we work with.
Practical information
Price
USD 180 per person
For each participating guest, as decided by the hosts.
Opening hours
Lunch Thursday to Sunday 12:30 pm to 13:00 pm (last seating) Closed on Monday and Tuesday
Where
Map
Website
Your Journey
Lunch at Sühring
USD 180 per person
Optional. Adding Sühring places a restaurant request in your journey for the guests who take part; Guest Relations arrange the table through your journey. It is a request — not a confirmed reservation, and availability is not guaranteed by this page. No date or time to choose here.

Sunday, 28 February 2027 · Vientiane
The Wedding
One day, four moments. The temple in the morning. Coffee & Cake on the return. The vows at the green door. Dinner poolside. Coffee & Cake, the Vow Ceremony and the Wedding Dinner are hosted by Haruthai & Suthep; Tak Bat remains a personal offering at the temple. Every answer here is yours alone.
01 · 08:00 – 12:00 · Wat Ong Teu, Vientiane
Temple Ceremony
Optional participation · Dress · Lao Traditional Dress
The Buddhist morning at Wat Ong Teu. Guests attending are welcome to take part in Tak Bat, the traditional offering of food to Buddhist monks — a personal offering, arranged individually on the morning.
More about Tak Bat
Peggy · you

A personal offering, prepared for you and presented by you at the Temple Ceremony. USD 15 · your cost, your choice alone.

Available once you have chosen to attend the Temple Ceremony.

SURFACE 159
==================================================
ROUTE: /wedding · More about Tak Bat
STATE: DRAWER OPEN
SOURCE: wedding.html TAKBAT
CONTEXT: the Tak Bat drawer

Close
Part of the Temple Ceremony
Morning Alms-Giving · Tak Bat
Guests attending the Temple Ceremony are welcome to take part in Tak Bat, the traditional offering of food to Buddhist monks — one of the oldest ways a day begins in Laos.
The offering is personal and arranged individually on the morning. Guest Relations will guide you; no fixed amount is set, and it is not the Sangkhathan.

Sunday, 28 February 2027 · and the travelling days
Wedding Preparation
Two things to prepare for the wedding day: what to wear, and where you will sit. The dress code is understood once, here, and confirmed once — in your own name.
01 · Required
Dress code
The wedding day carries two codes, and the travelling days carry a third. The photographs are references, not rules: they show the register, not a uniform. Nothing is bought or hired through this website.
Sunday, 28 February · Morning
Lao Traditional Dress
Temple Ceremony · 08:00 – 12:00 · Wat Ong Teu, Vientiane
The Buddhist morning is dressed in Lao tradition. Shoulders and knees are covered inside the temple, and shoes come off at the door. A sinh may be borrowed or made in Vientiane — Guest Relations helps anyone who would like that arranged.
[aria-label] Lao traditional dress references
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 1
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 2
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 3
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 4
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 5
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 6
Sunday, 28 February · Afternoon and evening
Black Tie
Coffee & Cake · Vow Ceremony · Wedding Dinner · Souphattra Heritage
From the return out of the temple, the day is black tie and stays black tie until the end of dinner. The courtyard is warm and the evening is long: a dinner jacket, a long dress, and shoes that survive a garden are the whole of it.
[aria-label] Black tie references
[alt] Black tie — reference 1
[alt] Black tie — reference 2
[alt] Black tie — reference 3
[alt] Black tie — reference 4
[alt] Black tie — reference 5
[alt] Black tie — reference 6
21 February – 08 March
Resort Wear
The travelling days · Bangkok · the night train · Vientiane · Kunming · Lijiang
Everything outside the wedding day is resort wear — easy, unhurried, and warm enough for a Lijiang evening at 2,400 metres. Comfortable shoes matter more than anything else on this list.
[aria-label] Resort wear references
[alt] Resort wear — reference 2
[alt] Resort wear — reference 3
[alt] Resort wear — reference 4
[alt] Resort wear — reference 5
[alt] Resort wear — reference 6

02 · Your seats · required for the events you attend
Your seats
Tap a chair on the plan, read the summary, confirm. First names show who already sits where, so you can find the people you know. A chair is held in your name only once you confirm it, and you can change it while seating stays open.

[page]
Sunday, 28 February 2027 · and the travelling days
Wedding Preparation
Two things to prepare for the wedding day: what to wear, and where you will sit. The dress code is understood once, here, and confirmed once — in your own name.
01 · Required
Dress code
The wedding day carries two codes, and the travelling days carry a third. The photographs are references, not rules: they show the register, not a uniform. Nothing is bought or hired through this website.
Sunday, 28 February · Morning
Lao Traditional Dress
Temple Ceremony · 08:00 – 12:00 · Wat Ong Teu, Vientiane
The Buddhist morning is dressed in Lao tradition. Shoulders and knees are covered inside the temple, and shoes come off at the door. A sinh may be borrowed or made in Vientiane — Guest Relations helps anyone who would like that arranged.
[aria-label] Lao traditional dress references
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 1
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 2
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 3
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 4
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 5
[alt] Lao traditional dress — reference 6
Sunday, 28 February · Afternoon and evening
Black Tie
Coffee & Cake · Vow Ceremony · Wedding Dinner · Souphattra Heritage
From the return out of the temple, the day is black tie and stays black tie until the end of dinner. The courtyard is warm and the evening is long: a dinner jacket, a long dress, and shoes that survive a garden are the whole of it.
[aria-label] Black tie references
[alt] Black tie — reference 1
[alt] Black tie — reference 2
[alt] Black tie — reference 3
[alt] Black tie — reference 4
[alt] Black tie — reference 5
[alt] Black tie — reference 6
21 February – 08 March
Resort Wear
The travelling days · Bangkok · the night train · Vientiane · Kunming · Lijiang
Everything outside the wedding day is resort wear — easy, unhurried, and warm enough for a Lijiang evening at 2,400 metres. Comfortable shoes matter more than anything else on this list.
[aria-label] Resort wear references
[alt] Resort wear — reference 2
[alt] Resort wear — reference 3
[alt] Resort wear — reference 4
[alt] Resort wear — reference 5
[alt] Resort wear — reference 6

02 · Your seats · required for the events you attend
Your seats
Tap a chair on the plan, read the summary, confirm. First names show who already sits where, so you can find the people you know. A chair is held in your name only once you confirm it, and you can change it while seating stays open.
